# Water Markets Data Standards 2026

I, Dr Stuart Minchin, Director of Meteorology, make the following standards.

Dated 30 June 2026

Dr Stuart Minchin

Director of Meteorology

Contents

### Part 1—Preliminary

###### 1 Name

This instrument is the *Water Markets Data Standards 2026*.

###### 2 Commencement

(1) Each provision of this instrument specified in column 1 of the table commences, or is taken to have commenced, in accordance with column 2 of the table. Any other statement in column 2 has effect according to its terms.

(2) Any information in column 3 of the table is not part of this instrument. Information may be inserted in this column, or information in it may be edited, in any published version of this instrument.

###### 3 Authority

This instrument is made under subsection 135J(1) of the *Water Act 2007*.

###### 4 Definitions

Note: A number of expressions used in this instrument are defined in the Act, including the following:

(a) eligible tradeable water right;

(b) eligible water markets intermediary;

(c) irrigation infrastructure operator;

(d) water market authority;

(e) water markets information.

In this instrument:

***Act*** means the *Water Act 2007*.

***assign a number or other identifier***, by a water market authority or trading platform operator to identify an eligible tradeable water right, a water account, a trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right or a buy or sell offer, includes to use a number or other identifier that has been assigned (however described) by another person or entity to identify the same thing.

***Basin State water market authority*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***batched transaction*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***category 1 water markets information*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***category 2 water markets information*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***category 3 water markets information*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***combined account*** means a water account in which a water market authority manages multiple water accounts (other than for the primary purpose of billing).

Note: Examples of combined accounts include accounts known as amalgamated accounts, linked accounts, merged accounts, single water allocation accounts, controlling entity accounts and single farming unit accounts.

***component trade or transfer*** of an eligible tradeable water right has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***data record*** means a record of a kind of water markets information made in accordance with a table in Schedule 1, 2 or 3.

Note: The following are examples of data records:

(a) a record containing the set of information in Schedule 1 that applies in relation to a permanent eligible tradeable water right on issue;

(b) a record containing the set of information in clause 1 of Schedule 2 that applies in relation to an individual trade or transfer;

(c) a record containing the set of information in Schedule 3 that applies in relation to an offer.

***destination water market authority***, in relation to the trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right, means the water market authority responsible for approving, allowing or registering the trade or transfer in the jurisdiction where the water right exists after the trade or transfer is finalised.

***finalised***, in relation to the trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right: see section 5.

***IIO bulk licence*** means a water access entitlement that is:

(a) held by an irrigation infrastructure operator; and

(b) assigned by the irrigation infrastructure operator to customers of the operator as irrigation rights held by the customers.

***IIO water market authority*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***metadata element***: see subsection 6(2).

***metadata entity***: see subsection 6(1).

***origin water market authority***, in relation to the trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right, means the water market authority responsible for approving, allowing or registering the trade or transfer in the jurisdiction where the water right exists before the trade or transfer is finalised.

***permanent eligible tradeable water right*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***Regulations*** means the *Water Regulations 2008*.

***relevant water register*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***representative***, of an owner, seller or buyer of an eligible tradeable water right, means an entity (other than an eligible water markets intermediary) that has the lawful authority (including under a delegation) to sign a trade application in relation to a trade or transfer of the eligible tradeable water right.

Examples: A lawyer, a power of attorney or a farm manager.

***subcategory 2a water markets information***: see paragraph 10(2)(a).

***subcategory 2b water markets information***: see paragraph 10(2)(b).

***subcategory 2c water markets information***: see paragraph 10(2)(c).

***temporary eligible tradeable water right*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***trade application*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***trading platform operator*** has the same meaning as in the Regulations.

***water account***, in relation to an eligible tradeable water right, means an account managed by a water market authority as a ledger for the water right in which the authority records information about the water right, including information about the trade or transfer of the water right.

***water markets trading zone*** means an area described in an item of the table in clause 13 of Schedule 5.

###### 5 Meaning of finalised

A trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right is ***finalised*** if:

(a) in the case of a trade or transfer to which item 5 of the table in subregulation 7A.07(1) of the Regulations applies—a water market authority takes an action mentioned in paragraph (c) of column 2 of that item in respect of the trade or transfer; or

(b) in the case of a trade or transfer to which item 6 of that table applies—a water market authority makes the decision mentioned in paragraph (b) of column 2 of that item in respect of the trade or transfer; or

(c) in the case of a trade or transfer to which item 7 of that table applies—an IIO water market authority takes an action mentioned in paragraph (b) of column 2 of that item in respect of the trade or transfer.

###### 6 Meaning of metadata entity and metadata element

(1) A ***metadata entity***, in relation to the kinds of water markets information required to be collected, generated or recorded, and given to the Bureau, is any of the following:

(a) an eligible tradeable water right;

(b) an owner of an eligible tradeable water right;

(c) a water account in relation to an eligible tradeable water right;

(d) a water markets trading zone;

(e) a trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right;

(f) a buyer or seller of an eligible tradeable water right;

(g) a representative of a buyer or seller of an eligible tradeable water right;

(h) an eligible water markets intermediary acting on behalf of a buyer or seller of an eligible tradeable water right in respect of the trade or transfer of that right;

(i) an individual trader of an eligible water markets intermediary;

(j) a trading platform;

(k) a water market authority;

(l) a buy or sell offer.

(2) A ***metadata element*** of a metadata entity is an attribute belonging to the set of attributes that describe the metadata entity.

Note: The metadata elements of metadata entities are specified in the separate items of the tables in Schedules 1, 2 and 3.

(3) Clause 1 of Schedule 4 lists the metadata elements of each metadata entity mentioned in subsection (1), and the category or subcategory of water markets information that each metadata element belongs to.

(4) Clause 2 of Schedule 4 includes a simplified diagram that shows the conceptual relationships between metadata entities.

###### 7 References to metadata elements

A metadata element specified in an item of a table in Schedule 1, 2 or 3 may be referred to by reference to the metadata element ID specified in column 1 of the item.

Example: Item 1 of the table in Schedule 1 specifies the metadata element that is the number or other identifier assigned by a water market authority for a permanent eligible tradeable water right. The metadata element ID for the metadata element is WM1‑ETID. A reference to WM1‑ETID in this instrument is a reference to the metadata element specified in item 1 of the table in Schedule 1.

### Part 2—Collecting, generating or recording water markets information

#### Division 1—Preliminary

###### 8 Application of this Part

This Part applies in relation to:

(a) a water market authority that is required by Division 4 of Part 7A of the Act to collect, generate or record category 1 water markets information or category 2 water markets information; and

(b) a trading platform operator that is required by Division 4 of Part 7A of the Act to collect, generate or record category 3 water markets information.

Note 1: For the requirement for a person to collect, generate or record water markets information, see section 135H of the Act.

Note 2: For the specification of the classes of persons required to collect, generate or record specified kinds of water markets information, see regulation 7A.09 of the Regulations.

#### Division 2—Main requirements

###### 9 Purpose of this Division

This Division:

(a) deals with the details for collecting, generating or recording water markets information, as required by Division 4 of Part 7A of the Act; and

(b) deals with the details of the persons or bodies that will be required to collect, generate or record such water markets information; and

(c) provides for metadata requirements in respect of such water markets information; and

(d) provides for the manner in which such water markets information is to be collected, generated or recorded.

###### 10 How water markets information is to be collected, generated or recorded

Category 1 water markets information

(1) A water market authority collects, generates or records category 1 water markets information about a permanent eligible tradeable water right in accordance with this instrument only if the authority collects, generates or records the information required by column 3 of each item of the table in Schedule 1 that applies in relation to the permanent eligible tradeable water right.

Category 2 water markets information

(2) A water market authority collects, generates or records category 2 water markets information about a trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right in accordance with this instrument only if the authority collects, generates or records, for each applicable individual trade or transfer identified in accordance with Division 3 of this Part, the following:

(a) if neither paragraph (b) nor (c) apply—the information (***subcategory 2a water markets information***) required by column 3 of each item of the table in clause 1 of Schedule 2 that applies in relation to the trade or transfer;

(b) if the trade or transfer is a component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right, and the water market authority is the IIO water market authority responsible for the batching of the component trade or transfer into a batched transaction—the information (***subcategory 2b water markets information***) required by column 3 of each item of the table in clause 2 of Schedule 2 that applies in relation to the trade or transfer;

(c) if:

(i) the trade or transfer is of a water access entitlement; and

(ii) the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority; and

(iii) the authority has entered information onto, or updated information on, a relevant water register to record a decision described in item 3 of the table in subregulation 7A.07(1) of the Regulations made about a trade application lodged with, or notified to, the authority in respect of the trade or transfer, or to record that the trade application is withdrawn, or has lapsed or expired;

the information (***subcategory 2c water markets information***) required by column 3 of each item of the table in clause 3 of Schedule 2 that applies in relation to the trade or transfer.

Category 3 water markets information

(3) A trading platform operator collects, generates or records category 3 water markets information about an offer published on a trading platform provided by the operator in accordance with this instrument only if the operator collects, generates or records the information required by column 3 of each item of the table in Schedule 3 that applies in relation to the offer.

###### 11 How identifying information is to be collected, generated or recorded

(1) This section applies if a water market authority or trading platform operator is required by Division 4 of Part 7A of the Act to collect, generate or record the following water markets information:

(a) the name of a metadata entity;

(b) the address of a metadata entity;

(c) the ACN, ARBN or ABN of a metadata entity.

Requirements for recording a name

(2) The water market authority or trading platform operator collects, generates or records the name of a metadata entity in accordance with this instrument by collecting, generating or recording the following:

(a) if the entity is:

(i) a company registered under the *Corporations Act 2001*; or

(ii) a registered body (within the meaning of the *Corporations Act 2001*);

the entity’s name, as specified in the entity’s certificate of registration as a company or a registered body under the *Corporations Act 2001*;

(b) if paragraph (a) does not apply, and the entity is a business with a business name registered on the Business Names Register under the *Business Names Registration Act 2011*—the entity’s registered business name, as recorded on the Business Names Register;

(c) if neither paragraph (a) nor (b) apply—the entity’s full legal name (other than a trading name).

Requirements for recording an entity’s address

(3) The water market authority or trading platform operator collects, generates or records the address of a metadata entity in accordance with this instrument by collecting, generating or recording the following:

(a) if the entity is:

(i) a company registered under the *Corporations Act 2001*; or

(ii) a registered body (within the meaning of the *Corporations Act 2001*);

the address of the entity’s registered office (within the meaning of the *Corporations Act 2001*);

(b) if paragraph (a) does not apply, and the entity is a business with a business name registered on the Business Names Register under the *Business Names Registration Act 2011*—the address of the entity’s principal place of business;

(c) if neither paragraph (a) nor (b) apply—the entity’s street address or, if there is no street address, the entity’s postal address.

Requirements for recording an entity’s ACN, ARBN or ABN

(4) The water market authority or trading platform operator collects, generates or records the ACN, ARBN or ABN of a metadata entity in accordance with this instrument by collecting, generating or recording the following:

(a) if the entity has an ACN—the ACN;

(b) if paragraph (a) does not apply, and the entity has an ARBN—the ARBN;

(c) if neither paragraph (a) nor (b) applies—the ABN.

Exception—names and addresses to be given to Bureau before 1 August 2027

(5) If:

(a) in accordance with the requirement in item 1 of the table in subregulation 7A.07(1) of the Regulations, a water market authority is required to give a name or address of an owner of a permanent eligible tradeable water right to the Bureau; and

(b) before 1 August 2027, the authority has not included a name or address of the owner on a relevant water register for information about the permanent eligible tradeable water right that meets the requirements of subsections (2) or (3) (as applicable);

then, despite those subsections, the authority may collect, generate or record the name or address in accordance with this instrument by collecting, generating or recording a name or address of the owner that is included on the relevant water register before 1 August 2027.

#### Division 3—Individual trades and transfers

###### 12 Purpose of this Division

For the purposes of subparagraph 135J(3)(b)(v) of the Act, this Division provides for the identification of individual trades or transfers of eligible tradeable water rights.

###### 13 Identifying individual trades and transfers

(1) A trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right is an individual trade or transfer of the eligible tradeable water right if any of the following paragraphs apply in relation to the trade or transfer:

(a) the trade or transfer involves a change in the ownership of the eligible tradeable water right, whether or not the trade or transfer involves a change of the water markets trading zone (or other place) in which the eligible tradeable water right is located;

(b) the trade or transfer requires the lodging of a trade application with a water market authority, whether or not the trade or transfer involves:

(i) a change in the ownership of the eligible tradeable water right; or

(ii) a change of the water markets trading zone (or other place) in which the eligible tradeable water right is located;

(c) the trade or transfer involves a change of the water markets trading zone in which the eligible tradeable water right is located, whether or not the trade or transfer involves a change in the ownership of the eligible tradeable water right;

(d) the trade or transfer establishes a lease arrangement or an arrangement providing for a temporary transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right;

(e) the trade or transfer establishes a tagged water access entitlement (within the meaning of the Basin Plan);

(f) the trade or transfer is taken to be an individual trade or transfer of a tradeable water right in accordance with another section of this Division.

(2) For the purposes of subsection (1), it is irrelevant whether the trade or transfer involves the payment of consideration.

###### 14 Trades and transfers resulting from certain accounting practices

(1) This section applies in relation to a water market authority that is:

(a) an infrastructure operator; or

(b) an irrigation infrastructure operator.

(2) A trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right that results from an accounting practice of the water market authority is taken to be an individual trade or transfer if it is:

(a) a purchase of a temporary eligible tradeable water right (typically water assigned against an irrigation right) by a customer of the water market authority in the circumstance that:

(i) an end of period reconciliation (also known as a forced balancing) of the customer’s water account relevant to that temporary eligible tradeable water right has found that the account is overdrawn; and

(ii) the water market authority requires the customer to make the purchase to rebalance the water account; or

(b) a purchase of a temporary eligible tradeable water right (typically water assigned against an irrigation right) by a customer of the water market authority as a result of the authority having excess water achieved through efficiency gains; or

(c) a trade or transfer of water between the water market authority’s customers outside of a combined account to or from the water market authority’s customers within a combined account.

(3) However, subsection (2) does not apply in relation to a trade or transfer resulting from any of the following accounting practices:

(a) the distribution of water for free to a customer of the water market authority in the circumstance that the excess water is achieved through efficiency gains;

(b) the transfer of water between the water market authority’s customers within a combined account.

###### 15 Batched transactions and component trades and transfers

Each of the following is taken to be an individual trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right:

(a) a batched transaction;

(b) a component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right.

Note 1: A Basin State water market authority is required to collect, generate or record subcategory 2a water markets information about a batched transaction: see clause 1 of Schedule 2.

Note 2: An IIO water market authority that is responsible for batching a component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right into a batched transaction is required to collect, generate or record subcategory 2b water markets information about the component trade or transfer: see clause 2 of Schedule 2.

#### Division 4—Unique identifiers

###### 16 Requirement for water market authorities to assign unique identifiers

(1) This section applies if:

(a) in accordance with Division 2 of this Part, a water market authority is required to collect, generate or record water markets information that is a number or other identifier (a ***unique identifier***) assigned by the authority to describe a metadata element of a metadata entity; and

(b) the metadata element of the metadata entity is:

(i) for a metadata entity that is an eligible tradeable water right or a water account in relation to an eligible tradeable water right—WM1‑ETID, WM2a‑ETID, WM2c‑ETID, WM1‑REID, WM2a‑IBEI, WM2a‑ISEI; WM1‑WAID, WM1‑CAID, WM2a‑BEWI, WM2c‑BEWI, WM2a‑SEWI or WM2b‑BSEW; or

(ii) for a metadata entity that is a trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right—WM2a‑TRID, WM2b‑TRID, WM2c‑TRID, WM2a‑OTID or WM2a‑DTID.

(2) The water markets information is collected, generated or recorded in accordance with this instrument only if:

(a) in the case that another water market authority has assigned a unique identifier as the value for the metadata element of the metadata entity—the unique identifier assigned by the authority as the value for the metadata element of the metadata entity is the same as that unique identifier; and

(b) if paragraph (a) does not apply—the unique identifier assigned by the authority as the value for the metadata element of the metadata entity is unique within the authority to the relevant eligible tradeable water right, water account or trade or transfer; and

(c) the unique identifier assigned by the authority in accordance with paragraph (a) or (b) is always used by the authority as the identifier for the relevant eligible tradeable water right, water account or trade or transfer.

###### 17 Requirement for trading platform operators to assign unique identifiers

(1) This section applies if:

(a) in accordance with Division 2 of this Part, a trading platform operator is required to collect, generate or record water markets information that is a number or other identifier (a ***unique identifier***) assigned by the operator for a metadata element of a metadata entity; and

(b) the metadata entity is a buy or sell offer; and

(c) the metadata element of the metadata entity is WM3‑OFID or WM3‑ROID.

(2) The water markets information is collected, generated or recorded in accordance with this instrument only if:

(a) the unique identifier assigned by the operator as the value for the metadata element of the metadata entity is unique within the operator to the relevant buy or sell offer; and

(b) the unique identifier assigned by the operator in accordance with paragraph (a) is always used by the operator in such information as the identifier for the buy or sell offer.

### Part 3—Providing water markets information to the Bureau

#### Division 1—Preliminary

###### 18 Application of this Part

This Part applies in relation to:

(a) a water market authority that is required by Division 4 of Part 7A of the Act to give to the Bureau a copy of category 1 water markets information or category 2 water markets information; and

(b) a trading platform operator that is required by Division 4 of Part 7A of the Act to give to the Bureau a copy of category 3 water markets information.

Note 1: For the requirement for a person to give water markets information to the Bureau, see section 135F of the Act.

Note 2: For the specification of the classes of persons required to give to the Bureau specified kinds of water markets information, see regulation 7A.07 of the Regulations.

#### Division 2—Main requirements

###### 19 Purpose of this Division

This Division:

(a) deals with the details for providing water markets information to the Bureau, as required by Division 4 of Part 7A of the Act; and

(b) provides for metadata requirements in respect of such water markets information; and

(c) provides for the form and manner in which such water markets information is to be provided to the Bureau.

###### 20 Form and manner in which copies of water markets information are to be given to the Bureau

(1) If a water market authority or trading platform operator is required to give a copy of water markets information to the Bureau, the copy must be provided in the form of a data record that meets the requirements of this section.

(2) The water market authority or trading platform operator must make a data record in accordance with the table in Schedule 1, 2 or 3 that is relevant to the kind of water markets information in the copy by recording, for each applicable item of the table, the following water markets information:

(a) the information the water market authority or trading platform operator collects, generates or records for the item in accordance with section 10;

(b) either or both of the following:

(i) the metadata element ID for the item;

(ii) the metadata element name for the item;

(c) the data or associated information in accordance with the data field type and data field pattern for the item.

(3) The data record must be provided to the Bureau:

(a) in the form of an allowable schema or template made available by the Bureau for the kind of water markets information included in the data record; or

(b) in a form that enables the data record to be uploaded or entered by the use of a facility made available by the Bureau.

Note: Examples for paragraph (a) of forms made available by the Bureau for different kinds of water markets information include schemas in the CSV (short for comma separated variable) format and the JSON (short for JavaScript object notation) format.

(4) The data record must be provided to the Bureau by:

(a) the use of SFTP (short for secure file transfer protocol); or

(b) the use of an API (short for application programming interface); or

(c) being uploaded or entered by the use of a facility made available by the Bureau.

(5) This section is subject to sections 21 and 22.

###### 21 Form and manner in which copies of corrected water markets information are to be given to the Bureau

(1) This section applies if a water market authority or trading platform operator:

(a) has given a copy of water markets information to the Bureau in the form of a data record (the ***first data record***); and

(b) has identified that information in the copy is incorrect; and

(c) is electing to give a copy of the updated information as a copy of corrected information.

(2) In giving a copy of the corrected information, the water market authority or trading platform operator must:

(a) give to the Bureau a second data record that:

(i) includes the value ‘Error cancel’ from the controlled list relevant to WM1‑ETST, WM2a‑TRST, WM2b‑TRST, WM2c‑TRST or WM3‑OFST (as applicable); and

(ii) otherwise is identical to the first data record; and

(b) give to the Bureau a third data record that:

(i) includes the corrected information for the metadata elements that contain the error; and

(ii) otherwise is identical to the first data record.

###### 22 Form and manner in which copies of certain updated subcategory 2a water markets information are to be given to the Bureau

(1) This section applies if:

(a) a water market authority has given a copy of subcategory 2a water markets information to the Bureau in the form of a data record (the ***first data record***) that includes information for WM2a‑DUOL (duration of lease or temporary transfer); and

(b) the lease or temporary transfer ends earlier than the period recorded in the first data record.

(2) In giving a copy of updated information to the Bureau, the water market authority must give to the Bureau a second data record that:

(a) includes the value ‘Early end to lease or temporary transfer’ from the controlled list relevant to WM2a‑TRST; and

(b) otherwise is identical to the first data record.

###### 23 Other matters

Controlled lists

(1) Schedule 5 lists the values for the controlled lists referred to in Schedules 1, 2 and 3.

(2) If column 6 of an item of a table in Schedule 1, 2 or 3 provides that the data field pattern for the metadata element specified in the item is a controlled list:

(a) a value from the controlled list must be recorded for the item; and

(b) if:

(i) the controlled list is “trade type”; and

(ii) more than one value is applicable; and

(iii) an applicable value is a value that is specific to a State;

a value that is applicable to the State must be recorded for the item.

Any

(3) If column 6 of an item of a table in Schedule 1, 2 or 3 provides that the data field pattern for the metadata element specified in the item is “Any”, water markets information may be recorded for the item in any combination of letters, numbers, characters and spaces.

Examples

(4) Schedule 6 includes examples for the recording of information for metadata elements in a data record.

## Schedule 1—Category 1 water markets information

Note: See subsection 10(1).

<td>water markets trading zone</td>
<td>The name of the water markets trading zone in which the permanent eligible tradeable water right exists</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:water markets trading zone</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>10</td>
<td>WM1‑TTRZ</td>
<td>tagged water markets trading zone</td>
<td>The name of the water markets trading zone against which the permanent eligible tradeable water right is tagged upon establishment of a tagged arrangement</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority; and<br>(b) the permanent eligible tradeable water right is a water access entitlement; and<br>(c) there is an establishment of a tagged arrangement</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:water markets trading zone</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>11</td>
<td>WM1‑WAID</td>
<td>water account ID related to ETWR</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the water market authority for:<br>(a) the water account most relevant to the permanent eligible tradeable water right; or<br>(b) if there is no such water account—the permanent eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>12</td>
<td>WM1‑CAID</td>
<td>combined account ID related to ETWR</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the water market authority for the combined account mentioned in paragraph (b) of column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the water market authority is an IIO water market authority; and<br>(b) the water account most relevant to the permanent eligible tradeable water right is managed in a combined account</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>13</td>
<td>WM1‑EONA</td>
<td>ETWR owner name</td>
<td>The name of each owner of the permanent eligible tradeable water right<br>See the note at the end of this clause.</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>14</td>
<td>WM1‑EOAD</td>
<td>ETWR owner address</td>
<td>The address of each owner of the permanent eligible tradeable water right<br>See the note at the end of this clause.</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>15</td>
<td>WM1‑EOAC</td>
<td>ETWR owner ACN, ARBN or ABN</td>
<td>The ACN, ARBN or ABN of each owner mentioned in column 4 of this item<br>See the note at the end of this clause.</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) an owner of the permanent eligible tradeable water right has an ACN, ARBN or ABN; and<br>(b) the water market authority has recorded the ACN, ARBN or ABN in a relevant water register for information about the permanent eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>9 or 11 digit number. Can include spaces between number blocks.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>16</td>
<td>WM1‑EOTY</td>
<td>ETWR owner type</td>
<td>A category for the type of owner of the permanent eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:owner type</td>
</tr>
</table>

Note: For WM1‑EONA, WM1‑EOAD and WM1‑EOAC: A water market authority may collect, generate or record ownership details at the water account level rather than at the permanent eligible tradeable water right level. In these circumstances, an owner of the water account for the permanent eligible tradeable water right would be an owner of the permanent eligible tradeable water right.

## Schedule 2—Category 2 water markets information

Note: See subsection 10(2).

###### 1 Requirements for collecting, generating or recording subcategory 2a water markets information—finalised trades or transfers (other than component trades or transfers)

The following table specifies the water markets information a water market authority must collect, generate or record about a trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right that:

(a) is an individual trade or transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right or a temporary eligible tradeable water right that is finalised; and

(b) is not a component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="7">Table: Subcategory 2a water markets information about a finalised trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Item</td>
<td>Column 1<br>Metadata element ID</td>
<td>Column 2<br>Metadata element name</td>
<td>Column 3<br>Metadata element description</td>
<td>Column 4<br>Requirement condition</td>
<td>Column 5<br>Data field type</td>
<td>Column 6<br>Data field pattern</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>1</td>
<td>WM2a‑TRID</td>
<td>trade ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the water market authority for the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td>WM2a‑TRST</td>
<td>trade status</td>
<td>The status relevant to the information recorded in this data record</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:trade status</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td>WM2a‑TTYP</td>
<td>trade type</td>
<td>The category for the type of trade or transfer<br>See Note 1 at the end of this clause</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:trade type</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td>WM2a‑QUTR</td>
<td>quantity traded</td>
<td>The share or volume in megalitres, or the extraction rate in megalitres per day, of water associated with the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>numeric</td>
<td>must be greater than or equal to zero. Up to 3 digits after the decimal place</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td>WM2a‑UOME</td>
<td>unit of measure</td>
<td>The measurement unit applicable to the quantity of water associated with the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:unit of measure</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>6</td>
<td>WM2a‑PPUT</td>
<td>price per unit traded</td>
<td>The total net price of the trade or transfer of the eligible tradeable water right, in Australian dollars (excluding fees and charges), divided by the quantity of water being traded or transferred</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>numeric</td>
<td>must be greater than or equal to zero. Up to 5 digits before the decimal place and up to 2 digits after the decimal place</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>7</td>
<td>WM2a‑RFTR</td>
<td>reason for trade</td>
<td>The primary reason for the trade or transfer, as stated in the trade application for the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:reason for trade</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>8</td>
<td>WM2a‑OTRZ</td>
<td>origin water markets trading zone</td>
<td>The name of the water markets trading zone in which the eligible tradeable water right existed before the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:water markets trading zone</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>9</td>
<td>WM2a‑DTRZ</td>
<td>destination water markets trading zone</td>
<td>The name of the water markets trading zone in which the eligible tradeable water right exists after the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:water markets trading zone</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>10</td>
<td>WM2a‑BEWI</td>
<td>buyer ETWR ID or water account ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier, assigned by the water market authority that manages the relevant water register for the information, for whichever of the following is owned by the buyer of the eligible tradeable water right and is most relevant to the trade or transfer:<br>(a) a permanent eligible tradeable water right;<br>(b) a water account for the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>11</td>
<td>WM2a‑SEWI</td>
<td>seller ETWR ID or water account ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier, assigned by the water market authority that manages the relevant water register for the information, for whichever of the following is owned by the seller of the eligible tradeable water right and is most relevant to the trade or transfer:<br>(a) a permanent eligible tradeable water right;<br>(b) a water account for the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>12</td>
<td>WM2a‑ETYP</td>
<td>ETWR type</td>
<td>The category for the type of eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:ETWR type</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>13</td>
<td>WM2a‑RTYP</td>
<td>resource type</td>
<td>The type of water resource that is relevant to the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:resource type</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>14</td>
<td>WM2a‑DTMT</td>
<td>matched trade date and time</td>
<td>The date, and time (if the time is recorded), that a price is agreed by a buyer and seller of the eligible tradeable water right for the trade or transfer<br>See Note 2 at the end of this clause</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>YYYY‑MM‑DDTHH:MM:SS±HH:MM</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>15</td>
<td>WM2a‑DLWA</td>
<td>date trade application lodged at water market authority</td>
<td>The date on which the trade application for the trade or transfer is lodged with, or notified to, the water market authority</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>YYYY‑MM‑DD</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>16</td>
<td>WM2a‑DAPR</td>
<td>date of approval or refusal</td>
<td>The date on which the water market authority makes a decision that has the effect that the trade or transfer is registered or approved (however described), or refused</td>
<td>Not required if the trade application for the trade or transfer is withdrawn</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>YYYY‑MM‑DD</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>17</td>
<td>WM2a‑DLRG</td>
<td>date trade application lodged at register</td>
<td>The date on which the trade application for the trade or transfer is lodged with, or notified to, the water market authority to be registered</td>
<td>Required if the eligible tradeable water right is a permanent eligible tradeable water right.<br>However, not required if the trade or transfer is refused</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>YYYY‑MM‑DD</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>18</td>
<td>WM2a‑DREG</td>
<td>date of registration</td>
<td>The date on which the water market authority updates the relevant water register for the information to reflect a decision that has the effect that the trade or transfer is registered (however described) as being in effect</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the eligible tradeable water right is a permanent eligible tradeable water right; and<br>(b) the trade or transfer is registered (however described) as being in effect</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>YYYY‑MM‑DD</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>19</td>
<td>WM2a‑ETID</td>
<td>ETWR ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the water market authority for the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Required if the eligible tradeable water right is a permanent eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>20</td>
<td>WM2a‑RLCT</td>
<td>reliability or licence class type</td>
<td>The category for the reliability or licence class which indicates the frequency with which water allocated against the eligible tradeable water right is able to be supplied in full</td>
<td>Required if the eligible tradeable water right is a permanent eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:reliability or licence class type</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>21</td>
<td>WM2a‑IBEI</td>
<td>individual buyer ETWR ID or water account ID—trade on to an IIO bulk licence</td>
<td>The number or other identifier, assigned by the IIO water market authority mentioned in paragraph (c) of column 4 of this item, for whichever of the following is owned by the buyer mentioned in that paragraph and is most relevant to the trade or transfer:<br>(a) a permanent eligible tradeable water right;<br>(b) a water account for the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority; and<br>(b) the eligible tradeable water right is a temporary eligible tradeable water right; and<br>(c) the buyer of the eligible tradeable water right is:<br>(i) an individual buyer; and<br>(ii) a customer of an IIO water market authority; and<br>(d) a seller of the eligible tradeable water right is external to the IIO water market authority mentioned in subparagraph (c)(ii)</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>22</td>
<td>WM2a‑ISEI</td>
<td>individual seller ETWR ID or water account ID—trade off of an IIO bulk licence</td>
<td>The number or other identifier, assigned by the IIO water market authority mentioned in paragraph (c) of column 4 of this item, for whichever of the following is owned by the seller mentioned in that paragraph and is most relevant to the trade or transfer:<br>(a) a permanent eligible tradeable water right;<br>(b) a water account for the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority; and<br>(b) the eligible tradeable water right is a temporary eligible tradeable water right; and<br>(c) the seller of the eligible tradeable water right is:<br>(i) an individual seller; and<br>(ii) a customer of an IIO water market authority; and<br>(d) a buyer of the eligible tradeable water right is external to the IIO water market authority mentioned in subparagraph (c)(ii)</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>23</td>
<td>WM2a‑BTIN</td>
<td>batched transaction indicator</td>
<td>Indicates whether the following statement is true or false: the trade or transfer is a batched transaction</td>
<td>Required if the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority</td>
<td>boolean</td>
<td>true or false</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>24</td>
<td>WM2a‑OWMA</td>
<td>origin water market authority name</td>
<td>The name of the origin water market authority in relation to the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority; and<br>(b) at least one other water market authority is involved in the trade or transfer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>25</td>
<td>WM2a‑DWMA</td>
<td>destination water market authority name</td>
<td>The name of the destination water market authority in relation to the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority; and<br>(b) at least one other water market authority is involved in the trade or transfer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>26</td>
<td>WM2a‑OTID</td>
<td>basin state origin trade ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the origin water market authority mentioned in paragraph (c) of column 4 of this item for the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority; and<br>(b) the trade or transfer is an interstate trade or transfer; and<br>(c) the origin water market authority in relation to the trade or transfer is a Basin State water market authority</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>27</td>
<td>WM2a‑DTID</td>
<td>basin state destination trade ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the destination water market authority mentioned in paragraph (c) of column 4 of this item for the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority; and<br>(b) the trade or transfer is an interstate trade or transfer; and<br>(c) the destination water market authority in relation to the trade or transfer is a Basin State water market authority</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>28</td>
<td>WM2a‑EXRT</td>
<td>exchange rate</td>
<td>A conversion that accounts for differences when the eligible tradeable water right is traded or transferred between water markets trading zones, including differences in transmission losses and changes in reliability</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the water market authority is a Basin State water market authority; and<br>(b) the eligible tradeable water right is a water access entitlement; and<br>(c) an exchange rate applies to the trade or transfer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>29</td>
<td>WM2a‑RFTO</td>
<td>reason for trade other</td>
<td>A description of the primary reason for the trade or transfer, as stated in the trade application for the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Required if the primary reason for the trade or transfer of the eligible tradeable water right is provided for WM2a‑RFTR as “other”</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>30</td>
<td>WM2a‑RFRL</td>
<td>reason for refusal</td>
<td>The reason for the refusal of the trade or transfer</td>
<td>Required if the water market authority refuses the trade or transfer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>31</td>
<td>WM2a‑DUOL</td>
<td>duration of lease or temporary transfer</td>
<td>The period (in years) of the lease arrangement or the arrangement providing for the temporary transfer</td>
<td>Required if the trade or transfer of the eligible tradeable water right establishes:<br>(a) a lease arrangement; or<br>(b) an arrangement providing for a temporary transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>numeric</td>
<td>must be greater than zero. Up to 3 digits before the decimal place and up to 2 digits after the decimal place</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>32</td>
<td>WM2a‑BNAM</td>
<td>buyer name</td>
<td>The name of each buyer of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Not required if the information:<br>(a) has been provided for WM1‑EONA; and<br>(b) is able to be linked to WM2a‑BNAM by the provision of information for WM2a‑BEWI and either WM1‑ETID or WM1‑WAID</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>33</td>
<td>WM2a‑BADD</td>
<td>buyer address</td>
<td>The address of each buyer of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Not required if the information:<br>(a) has been provided for WM1‑EOAD; and<br>(b) is able to be linked to WM2a‑BADD by the provision of information for WM2a‑BEWI and either WM1‑ETID or WM1‑WAID</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>34</td>
<td>WM2a‑BACN</td>
<td>buyer ACN, ARBN or ABN</td>
<td>The ACN, ARBN or ABN of each buyer mentioned in column 4 of this item of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Required if a buyer of the eligible tradeable water right has an ACN, ARBN or ABN.<br>However, not required if the information:<br>(a) has been provided for WM1‑EOAC; and<br>(b) is able to be linked to WM2a‑BACN by the provision of the information for WM2a‑BEWI and either WM1‑ETID or WM1‑WAID</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>9 or 11 digit number. Can include spaces between number blocks</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>35</td>
<td>WM2a‑BRPN</td>
<td>buyer representative name</td>
<td>The name of each representative mentioned in column 4 of this item<br>See Note 3 at the end of this clause</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) a representative is acting on behalf of a buyer of the eligible tradeable water right in respect of the trade or transfer; and<br>(b) the buyer has not signed the trade application for the trade or transfer; and<br>(c) the representative has signed the trade application for the trade or transfer on behalf of the buyer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>36</td>
<td>WM2a‑BRPA</td>
<td>buyer representative address</td>
<td>The address of each representative mentioned in column 4 of this item<br>See Note 3 at the end of this clause</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) a representative is acting on behalf of a buyer of the eligible tradeable water right in respect of the trade or transfer; and<br>(b) the buyer has not signed the trade application for the trade or transfer; and<br>(c) the representative has signed the trade application for the trade or transfer on behalf of the buyer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>37</td>
<td>WM2a‑SNAM</td>
<td>seller name</td>
<td>The name of each seller of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Not required if the information:<br>(a) has been provided for WM1‑EONA; and<br>(b) is able to be linked to WM2a‑SNAM by the provision of information for WM2a‑SEWI and either WM1‑ETID or WM1‑WAID</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>38</td>
<td>WM2a‑SADD</td>
<td>seller address</td>
<td>The address of each seller of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Not required if the information:<br>(a) has been provided for WM1‑EOAD; and<br>(b) is able to be linked to WM2a‑SADD by the provision of information for WM2a‑SEWI and either WM1‑ETID or WM1‑WAID</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>39</td>
<td>WM2a‑SACN</td>
<td>seller ACN, ARBN or ABN</td>
<td>The ACN, ARBN or ABN of each seller mentioned in column 4 of this item of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Required if a seller of the eligible tradeable water right has an ACN, ARBN or ABN.<br>However, not required if the information:<br>(a) has been provided for WM1‑EOAC; and<br>(b) is able to be linked to WM2a‑SACN by the provision of information for WM2a‑SEWI and either WM1‑ETID or WM1‑WAID</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>9 or 11 digit number. Can include spaces between number blocks</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>40</td>
<td>WM2a‑SRPN</td>
<td>seller representative name</td>
<td>The name of each representative mentioned in column 4 of this item<br>See Note 3 at the end of this clause</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) a representative is acting on behalf of a seller of the eligible tradeable water right in respect of the trade or transfer; and<br>(b) the seller has not signed the trade application for the trade or transfer; and<br>(c) the representative has signed the trade application for the trade or transfer on behalf of the seller</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>41</td>
<td>WM2a‑SRPA</td>
<td>seller representative address</td>
<td>The address of each representative mentioned in column 4 of this item<br>See Note 3 at the end of this clause</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) a representative is acting on behalf of a seller of the eligible tradeable water right in respect of the trade or transfer; and<br>(b) the seller has not signed the trade application for the trade or transfer; and<br>(c) the representative has signed the trade application for the trade or transfer on behalf of the seller</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>42</td>
<td>WM2a‑BIIN</td>
<td>buyer individual intermediary name</td>
<td>The name of the individual trader who facilitated the trade or transfer on behalf of the buyer of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the trade or transfer on behalf of the buyer of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>43</td>
<td>WM2a‑BION</td>
<td>buyer intermediary organisation name</td>
<td>The name of the eligible water markets intermediary mentioned in column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the trade or transfer on behalf of the buyer of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>44</td>
<td>WM2a‑BIOA</td>
<td>buyer intermediary organisation ACN, ARBN or ABN</td>
<td>The ACN, ARBN or ABN of the eligible water markets intermediary mentioned in column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the trade or transfer on behalf of the buyer of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>9 or 11 digit number. Can include spaces between number blocks</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>45</td>
<td>WM2a‑SIIN</td>
<td>seller individual intermediary name</td>
<td>The name of the individual trader who facilitated the trade or transfer on behalf of the seller of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the trade or transfer on behalf of the seller of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>46</td>
<td>WM2a‑SION</td>
<td>seller intermediary organisation name</td>
<td>The name of the eligible water markets intermediary mentioned in column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the trade or transfer on behalf of the seller of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>47</td>
<td>WM2a‑SIOA</td>
<td>seller intermediary organisation ACN, ARBN or ABN</td>
<td>The ACN, ARBN or ABN of the eligible water markets intermediary mentioned in column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the trade or transfer on behalf of the seller of the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>9 or 11 digit number. Can include spaces between number blocks</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>48</td>
<td>WM2a‑MTID</td>
<td>matched trade ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the eligible water markets intermediary mentioned in column 4 of this item for the matched trade or transfer<br>See Note 4 at the end of this clause</td>
<td>Required if the trade or transfer is facilitated by an eligible water markets intermediary</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>49</td>
<td>WM2a‑TPFN</td>
<td>trading platform name</td>
<td>The name assigned by the trading platform operator mentioned in column 4 of this item for the trading platform mentioned in that column</td>
<td>Required if a trading platform operator facilitates the trade or transfer on a trading platform</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
</table>

Note 1: For WM2a‑TTYP, the category for the type of trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right is also known as the dealing type for the trade or transfer.

Note 2: For WM2a‑DTMT, the date referred to is also known as the strike date. Examples for when a price is agreed to include the following:

(a) the date and time that a buy or sell offer is matched on a trading platform by the acceptance of the offer;

(b) the date of the latest signature of a buyer or seller on a trade application made in relation to the trade or transfer of the eligible tradeable water right.

Note 3: For WM2a‑BRPN, WM2a‑BRPA, WM2a‑SRPN and WM2a‑SRPA, for the definition of ***representative***, see section 4 of this instrument. Examples of representatives include lawyers, farm managers and persons acting under the authority of a power of attorney. An eligible water markets intermediary (including a trading platform operator) is not a representative.

Note 4: For WM2a‑MTID, the number or other identifier is also known as a broker reference number.

###### 2 Requirements for collecting, generating or recording subcategory 2b water markets information—finalised component trades or transfers

The following table specifies the water markets information an IIO water market authority must collect, generate or record about a component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right:

(a) that is an individual trade or transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right or a temporary eligible tradeable water right that is finalised; and

(b) in respect of which the IIO water market authority has batched the component trade or transfer into a batched transaction.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="7">Table: Subcategory 2b water markets information about a finalised component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right</td>
</tr>

<td>Item</td>
<td>Column 1<br>Metadata element ID</td>
<td>Column 2<br>Metadata element name</td>
<td>Column 3<br>Metadata element description</td>
<td>Column 4<br>Requirement condition</td>
<td>Column 5<br>Data field type</td>
<td>Column 6<br>Data field pattern</td>
</tr>

<td>1</td>
<td>WM2b‑TRID</td>

<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td>WM2b‑TRST</td>
<td>trade status</td>

<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:trade status</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td>WM2b‑QUTR</td>
<td>quantity traded</td>
<td>The share or volume in megalitres, or the extraction rate in megalitres per day, of water associated with the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>numeric</td>
<td>must be greater than or equal to zero. Up to 3 digits after the decimal place</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td>WM2b‑UOME</td>
<td>unit of measure</td>
<td>The measurement unit applicable to the quantity of water associated with the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:unit of measure</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td>WM2b‑PPUT</td>
<td>price per unit traded</td>
<td>The total net price of the eligible tradeable water right, in Australian dollars (excluding fees and charges), divided by the quantity of water being traded or transferred</td>
<td>Required if the price per unit paid or received by a buyer or seller of a component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right in respect of the batched transaction is different from the price per unit paid or received by a buyer or seller of any other component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right in respect of the batched transaction</td>

<td>6</td>

<td>Required if the primary reason for a component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right in respect of the batched transaction is different from the primary reason for any other component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right in respect of the batched transaction</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:reason for trade</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>7</td>
<td>WM2b‑BSEW</td>
<td>buyer or seller ETWR ID or water account ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the IIO water market authority for whichever of the following is owned by the buyer or seller (as applicable) of the eligible tradeable water right and is most relevant to the component trade or transfer:<br>(a) a permanent eligible tradeable water right;<br>(b) a water account for the eligible tradeable water right</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>8</td>
<td>WM2b‑RFTO</td>
<td>reason for trade other</td>
<td>A description of the primary reason for the component trade or transfer, as stated by the buyer or seller of the eligible tradeable water right in the trade application relevant to the component trade or transfer</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the primary reason for a component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right in respect of the batched transaction is different from the primary reason for any other component trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right in respect of the batched transaction; and<br>(b) the primary reason for the component trade or transfer is provided for WM2b‑RFTR as “other”</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
</table>

Note 2: For WM2c‑DTMT: The date referred to is also known as the strike date. Examples for when a price is agreed to include the following:

(a) the date and time that a buy or sell offer is matched on a trading platform by the acceptance of the offer;

(b) the date of the latest signature of a buyer or seller on a trade application made in relation to the trade or transfer of the eligible tradeable water right.

Note 3: For WM2c‑MTID: The number or other identifier is also known as the broker reference number.

## Schedule 3—Category 3 water markets information

Note: See subsection 10(3).

###### 1 Requirements for collecting, generating or recording category 3 water markets information

The following table specifies the water markets information a trading platform operator must collect, generate or record about an offer published on a trading platform provided by the operator in respect of a water allocation. The offer may be a buy offer or a sell offer.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="7">Table: Category 3 water markets information about an offer published on a trading platform provided by a trading platform operator in respect of a water allocation</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Item</td>
<td>Column 1<br>Metadata element ID</td>
<td>Column 2<br>Metadata element name</td>
<td>Column 3<br>Metadata element description</td>
<td>Column 4<br>Requirement condition</td>
<td>Column 5<br>Data field type</td>
<td>Column 6<br>Data field pattern</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>1</td>
<td>WM3‑OFID</td>
<td>offer ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the trading platform operator for the offer</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td>WM3‑ROID</td>
<td>related offer ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier assigned by the trading platform operator for an earlier offer mentioned in column 4 of this item<br>See Note 1 at the end of this clause</td>
<td>Required if the trading platform operator has generated or recorded a relationship between the offer and an earlier offer<br>However, not required if the number or other identifier of the offer and the earlier offer are the same</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td>WM3‑OFST</td>
<td>offer status</td>
<td>The status relevant to the information recorded in this data record</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:offer status</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td>WM3‑OTYP</td>
<td>offer type</td>
<td>The category for the type of offer</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:offer type</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td>WM3‑OLIN</td>
<td>offer link</td>
<td>A hyperlink to the offer on the trading platform</td>
<td>Optional</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>must be a valid URL</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>6</td>
<td>WM3‑QUOF</td>
<td>quantity offered</td>
<td>The volume of water, in megalitres, associated with the offer</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>numeric</td>
<td>must be greater than zero. Up to 3 digits after the decimal place.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>7</td>
<td>WM3‑OFSP</td>
<td>offer split</td>
<td>Indicates whether the following statement is true or false: the buyer or seller is willing to split the offer and buy or sell a portion of the quantity offered</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>boolean</td>
<td>true or false</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>8</td>
<td>WM3‑MSPQ</td>
<td>minimum split quantity</td>
<td>The minimum volume of water, in megalitres, that the buyer or seller is willing to split from the quantity offered</td>
<td>Required if:<br>(a) the information provided for WM3‑OFSP is “true”; and<br>(b) the trading platform operator records a minimum split quantity for the water that is the subject of the offer</td>
<td>numeric</td>
<td>must be greater than zero. Up to 3 digits after the decimal place.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>9</td>
<td>WM3‑BOPR</td>
<td>buy offer price per ML</td>
<td>The total net price of the water sought to be purchased in Australian dollars (excluding fees and charges) divided by the quantity of that water<br>See Note 2 at the end of this clause</td>
<td>Required if the offer is a buy offer</td>
<td>numeric</td>
<td>must be greater than zero. Up to 4 digits before the decimal place and up to 2 digits after the decimal place</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>10</td>
<td>WM3‑SOPR</td>
<td>sell offer price per ML</td>
<td>The total net price of the water offered for sale in Australian dollars (excluding fees and charges) divided by the quantity of that water</td>
<td>Required if the offer is a sell offer</td>
<td>numeric</td>
<td>must be greater than zero. Up to 4 digits before the decimal place and up to 2 digits after the decimal place</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>11</td>
<td>WM3‑OFDT</td>
<td>offer date and time</td>
<td>The date and time that the offer is published or changed on the trading platform</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>YYYY‑MM‑DDTHH:MM:SS±HH:MM</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>12</td>
<td>WM3‑MTRZ</td>
<td>water markets trading zone</td>
<td>The name of the water markets trading zone:<br>(a) in the case of a sell offer—in which the water allocation exists; or<br>(b) in the case of a buy offer—that the buyer wants to purchase the water allocation into</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:water markets trading zone</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>13</td>
<td>WM3‑RTYP</td>
<td>resource type</td>
<td>The type of water resource that is relevant to the water allocation</td>
<td>Always required</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>controlled list:resource type</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>14</td>
<td>WM3‑BSEW</td>
<td>buyer or seller ETWR ID or water account ID</td>
<td>The number or other identifier, assigned by the water market authority that manages the relevant water register for the information, for whichever of the following is most relevant to the offer:<br>(a) the permanent eligible tradeable water right mentioned in paragraph (a) of column 4;<br>(b) the water account mentioned in paragraph (b) of column 4</td>
<td>Required if a buyer or seller making the offer is the owner of:<br>(a) a permanent eligible tradeable water right that is relevant to the offer; or<br>(b) a water account for the water allocation that is relevant to the offer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>15</td>
<td>WM3‑BSNA</td>
<td>buyer or seller name</td>
<td>The name of each buyer or seller making the offer</td>
<td>Not required if the information has been provided for WM3‑BSEW</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>16</td>
<td>WM3‑BSAD</td>
<td>buyer or seller address</td>
<td>The address of each buyer or seller making the offer</td>
<td>Not required if the information has been provided for WM3‑BSEW</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>17</td>
<td>WM3‑BSAC</td>
<td>buyer or seller ACN, ARBN or ABN</td>
<td>The ACN, ARBN or ABN of each buyer or seller mentioned in column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if a buyer or seller making the offer has an ACN, ARBN or ABN<br>However, not required if the information has been provided for WM3‑BSEW</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>9 or 11 digit number. Can include spaces between number blocks.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>18</td>
<td>WM3‑IINA</td>
<td>individual intermediary name</td>
<td>The name of the individual trader who facilitated the making of the offer on behalf of a buyer or seller mentioned in column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the making of the offer on behalf of a buyer or seller making the offer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>19</td>
<td>WM3‑IONA</td>
<td>intermediary organisation name</td>
<td>The name of the eligible water markets intermediary mentioned in column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the making of the offer on behalf of a buyer or seller making the offer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>20</td>
<td>WM3‑IOAD</td>
<td>intermediary organisation address</td>
<td>The address of the eligible water markets intermediary mentioned in column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the making of the offer on behalf of a buyer or seller making the offer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>Any</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>21</td>
<td>WM3‑IOAC</td>
<td>intermediary organisation ACN, ARBN or ABN</td>
<td>The ACN, ARBN or ABN of the eligible water markets intermediary mentioned in column 4 of this item</td>
<td>Required if an eligible water markets intermediary facilitates the making of the offer on behalf of a buyer or seller making the offer</td>
<td>text</td>
<td>9 or 11 digit number. Can include spaces between number blocks.</td>
</tr>
</table>

Note 1: For WM3‑ROID: A relationship between an offer and an earlier offer may be generated or recorded by the trading platform operator if the offer is made when certain characteristics of the earlier offer, such as the price per megalitre or quantity offered, are amended.

Note 2: For WM3‑BOPR: The net price must be determined on the basis that the buy offer is filled by a seller in the water markets trading zone in which the water is being sought to purchase.
