## Schedule 5—Controlled lists

Note: See subsection 23(1).

###### 1 Controlled lists

This Schedule provides the controlled lists that are relevant to certain metadata elements specified in Schedules 1, 2 and 3.

###### 2 Controlled list—ETWR type

The following table specifies the values for the controlled list “ETWR type”.

Note: The values describe different types of eligible tradeable water rights.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Table: Controlled list—ETWR type</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Item</td>
<td>Column 1<br><b>Controlled list value</b></td>
<td>Column 2<br><b>Description</b></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Permanent eligible tradeable water right types</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>1</td>
<td>Water access entitlement</td>
<td>A permanent eligible tradeable water right that is a water access entitlement<br>Without limitation, this value includes the following:<br>(a) a water share issued under the <i>Water Act 1989</i> (Vic);<br>(b) a water access licence issued under the <i>Water Management Act 2000</i> (NSW);<br>(c) a water licence issued under the <i>Landscape South Australia Act 2019</i> (SA);<br>(d) a water allocation issued under the <i>Water Act 2000</i> (Qld);<br>(e) a water access entitlement issued under the <i>Water Resources Act 2000</i> (ACT)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td>Irrigation right</td>
<td>A permanent eligible tradeable water right that is an irrigation right</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td>Capped extraction share</td>
<td>A permanent eligible tradeable water right that is a right to a capped extraction share of the total amount of water that can be drawn from a watercourse at a certain point over a particular period of time<br>Without limitation, this value includes the following:<br>(a) an individual daily extraction component, within the meaning of the <i>Water Management Act 2000</i> (NSW);<br>(b) a notional rationing rate, within the meaning of the <i>Water Act 1989</i> (Vic.)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td>Water delivery right</td>
<td>A permanent eligible tradeable water right that is a water delivery right, water delivery share or water delivery entitlement</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td>Infrastructure operator water right</td>
<td>A permanent eligible tradeable water right that is a right (other than a water access right or water delivery right) that a person has against an infrastructure operator (other than an IIO water market authority) to receive water</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>6</td>
<td>Vic take and use licence</td>
<td>A permanent eligible tradeable water right that is a right to take and use all or part of a volume of water that is conferred by a licence issued under the <i>Water Act 1989</i> (Vic.)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Temporary eligible tradeable water right types</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>7</td>
<td>Water allocation</td>
<td>A temporary eligible tradeable water right that is a water allocation<br>Without limitation, this value includes seasonal water assignments to a water access entitlement (known as a water allocation) in Queensland</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>8</td>
<td>Water assigned against an irrigation right</td>
<td>A temporary eligible tradeable water right that is water assigned against an irrigation right for use, trade or transfer by the owner of the right during a given water accounting period<br>Note: Also known as an irrigation right allocation</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>9</td>
<td>Water delivered under an assignment of a water delivery right</td>
<td>A temporary eligible tradeable water right that is a right to delivery of a volume of water assigned against a water delivery right in a given water accounting period<br>Note: Also known as a temporary delivery entitlement or water allowance</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>10</td>
<td>Water assigned against an infrastructure operator water right</td>
<td>A temporary eligible tradeable water right that is water assigned against a right that a person has against an infrastructure operator (other than an IIO water market authority) to receive water and that is not a water access right or water delivery right<br>Note: Also known as an IO allocation</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>11</td>
<td>Water assigned against a Qld water licence</td>
<td>A temporary eligible tradeable water right that is a seasonal water assignment against a water licence (within the meaning of the <i>Water Act 2000</i> (Qld))</td>
</tr>
</table>

Note: For the definitions of ***irrigation right***, ***water access entitlement***, ***water access right***, ***water allocation*** and ***water delivery right***, see section 4 of the Act.

###### 3 Controlled list—trade status

The following table specifies the values for the controlled list “trade status”.

Note: The values describe the status of a trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Table: Controlled list—trade status</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Item</td>
<td>Column 1<br><b>Controlled list value</b></td>
<td>Column 2<br><b>Description</b></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>1</td>
<td>Trade application lodged</td>
<td>The trade application in respect of the trade or transfer of the water access entitlement is lodged with, or notified to, a Basin State water market authority and the authority has taken an action mentioned in paragraph (b) of column 2 of item 3 of the table in subregulation 7A.07(1) of the Regulations<br>This status is only relevant to subcategory 2c water markets information</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td>Approved or registered</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of the eligible tradeable water right is registered or approved (however described) and finalised by a water market authority and (as applicable):<br>(a) the authority has taken an action mentioned in paragraph (c) of column 2 of item 5 of the table in subregulation 7A.07(1) of the Regulations; or<br>(b) an IIO water market authority has taken an action mentioned in paragraph (b) of column 2 of item 7 of that table<br>This status is only relevant to subcategory 2a and 2b water markets information</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td>Refused</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of the eligible tradeable water right is refused and finalised by a water market authority and (as applicable):<br>(a) the authority has made the decision mentioned in paragraph (b) of column 2 of item 6 of the table in subregulation 7A.07(1) of the Regulations; or<br>(b) an IIO water market authority has taken an action mentioned in paragraph (b) of column 2 of item 7 of that table<br>This status is only relevant to subcategory 2a and 2b water markets information</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td>Withdrawn</td>
<td>The trade application in respect of the trade or transfer of the water access entitlement is withdrawn after it was lodged with, or notified to, a Basin state water market authority and before the authority made a decision to register or approve (however described), or to refuse, the trade or transfer<br>This status is only relevant to subcategory 2c water markets information<br>Note: This reflects the status of the trade or transfer at the time the authority takes the action mentioned in paragraph (c) of column 2 of item 4 of the table in subregulation 7A.07(1) of the Regulations in response to the application being withdrawn</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td>Lapsed or expired</td>
<td>The trade application in respect of the trade or transfer of the water access entitlement that has been lodged with, or notified to, a Basin State water market authority lapses or expires<br>This status is only relevant to subcategory 2c water markets information<br>Note: This reflects the status of the trade or transfer at the time the authority takes the action mentioned in paragraph (c) of column 2 of item 4 of the table in subregulation 7A.07(1) of the Regulations in response to the application lapsing or expiring</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>6</td>
<td>Early end to lease or temporary transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer that establishes a lease arrangement or an arrangement providing for the temporary transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right ends, or is terminated, earlier than the fixed period specified in the data record made in respect of the finalised trade or transfer<br>This status is only relevant to subcategory 2a water markets information</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>7</td>
<td>Error cancel</td>
<td>The previously provided data record contains an error<br>This status is relevant to subcategory 2a, 2b and 2c water markets information</td>
</tr>
</table>

###### 4 Controlled list—reason for trade

The following table specifies the values for the controlled list “reason for trade”.

Note: The values describe the principal reasons for the trades or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right, as identified in the trade application lodged with, or notified to, a water market authority for the trade or transfer.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Table: Controlled list—reason for the trade or transfer of eligible tradeable water right</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Item</td>
<td>Column 1<br><b>Controlled list value</b></td>
<td>Column 2<br><b>Description</b></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>1</td>
<td>Standard commercial</td>
<td>The trade or transfer:<br>(a) is for the purpose of an agreement or contract between 2 unrelated parties that is of a standard commercial nature; and<br>(b) is not part of, or included in, the sale of land</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td>Forward contract</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is for the purpose of delivering a water allocation associated with a forward contract made on a previous date</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td>Carryover parking</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is for the purpose of the temporary movement of a water allocation in a water accounting period to a water account to be held and carried over to a following water accounting period</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td>Carryover parking return</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is for the purpose of returning a water allocation that has been held and carried over in a water account as mentioned in item 3 to the water account from which it was originally moved</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td>Related party</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right in the circumstance that there is a relationship between the parties to the trade or transfer, and as a consequence the trade or transfer is not standard commercial in nature due to the relationship between the parties impacting the agreed price<br>Without limitation, this value includes a trade or transfer between family members or related corporate entities</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>6</td>
<td>Environmental</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is to a buyer who is classified as an environmental water holder or manager<br>Without limitation, this value includes a trade or transfer in the circumstance that no consideration is paid</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>7</td>
<td>Lease or temporary transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is associated with a lease or temporary transfer arrangement that passes the benefits and responsibilities associated with holding a permanent eligible tradeable water right to another person for a fixed period<br>Without limitation, this value includes a temporary transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right (also known as a term transfer, limited period transfer or limited term transfer)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>8</td>
<td>Private lease arrangement</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is for the purpose of fulfilling private lease arrangements made on a previous date, by delivery of an agreed amount of water at intervals over a defined period of time<br>This value does not include a temporary transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>9</td>
<td>Allocation trade as part of an entitlement trade</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a temporary eligible tradeable water right associated with the sale of a permanent eligible tradeable water right<br>Note 1: The combining of the trade or transfer of the temporary eligible tradable water right with the trade or transfer of the permanent eligible tradeable water right is also known as a “wet trade”<br>Note 2: The trade or transfer is of the temporary eligible tradeable water right that has already been allocated or assigned to the permanent eligible tradeable water right (or the remaining unused water in a water account) and is traded or transferred at the time of the trade or transfer of the permanent eligible tradeable water right</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>10</td>
<td>Trade as part of property sale</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is part of, or included in, the sale of land</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>11</td>
<td>Impaired sale or transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is at a price that may be reduced due to an impairment (for example, a company winding up, a deceased estate, a bankruptcy, a mortgagee in possession)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>12</td>
<td>Tagged trade establishment</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is of a water access entitlement between legal entities of different water sharing plans where the entitlement remains subjected to the rules of the water sharing plan from where the entitlement was sold</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>13</td>
<td>Government program</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is associated with a Commonwealth or State government program, other than a trade or transfer mentioned in item 6</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>14</td>
<td>Forced balancing</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is an end of period reconciliation<br>Note: An end of period reconciliation occurs when a customer of an IIO water market authority is required by the authority to “make good” or purchase water to rebalance a water account of the customer that is overdrawn</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>15</td>
<td>Call option</td>
<td>The trade or transfer fulfils or executes a call option as part of an agreement or contract that grants the right, but not the obligation, to buy water at a fixed price within a set period</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>16</td>
<td>Deferred delivery</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is for the purpose of an agreement or contract to purchase water that provides for the delivery and payment to be made on a specified future date</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>17</td>
<td>Transformation</td>
<td>The trade or transfer is for the purpose of a transformation<br>Note: A transformation is the process undertaken in accordance with water market rules: see section 97 of the Act and the <i>Water Market Rules 2009</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>18</td>
<td>Other</td>
<td>For a reason not mentioned in any of the other items of this table</td>
</tr>
</table>

###### 6 Controlled list—resource type

The following table specifies the values for the controlled list “resource type”.

</tr>

</tr>

</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>

<td>A surface water resource in which the water flow is not controlled through the use of infrastructure to store and release water</td>
</tr>

<td>Groundwater</td>
<td>A groundwater resource</td>
</tr>
</table>

###### 8 Controlled list—trade type

The following table specifies the values for the controlled list “trade type”.

Note: The values describe the type of trade or transfer of an eligible tradeable water right.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Table: Controlled list—trade type</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Item</td>
<td>Column 1<br><b>Controlled list value</b></td>
<td>Column 2<br><b>Description</b></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">General</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>1</td>
<td>Permanent transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right that is:<br>(a) a water access entitlement; or<br>(b) an irrigation right; or<br>(c) a capped extraction share; or<br>(d) a water delivery right; or<br>(e) a Vic take and use licence<br>Note: This value is relevant to Victoria, the Australian Capital Territory, and IIO water market authorities in New South Wales and South Australia</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td>Internal temporary transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a temporary eligible tradeable water right that is:<br>(a) water assigned against an irrigation right; or<br>(b) water delivered under an assignment of a water delivery right<br>Note: This value is relevant to IIO water market authorities in New South Wales and South Australia</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td>Allocation trade</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a temporary eligible tradeable water right that is a water allocation<br>Note: This value is relevant to Basin State water market authorities in Victoria and South Australia</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td>IIO lease</td>
<td>The trade or transfer establishes a lease arrangement or an arrangement providing for the temporary transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right that is an irrigation right or a water delivery right to a lessee for a fixed period<br>Note: This value is relevant to IIO water market authorities in New South Wales and South Australia</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td>Other</td>
<td>For a trade type not mentioned in any of the other items of this table</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">New South Wales</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>6</td>
<td>71M</td>
<td>The transfer of an access licence in accordance with section 71M of the <i>Water Management Act 2000</i> (NSW)<br>Note: Also known as a water access licence transfer with land</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>7</td>
<td>71N</td>
<td>The term transfer of water entitlements under an access licence within the meaning under section 71N of the <i>Water Management Act 2000</i> (NSW)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>8</td>
<td>71Q</td>
<td>The assignment of rights between access licences in accordance with section 71Q of the <i>Water Management Act 2000</i> (NSW)<br>Note: Also known as assigning share components between water access licences</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>9</td>
<td>71T</td>
<td>The assignment of water allocations between access licences in accordance with section 71T of the <i>Water Management Act 2000</i> (NSW)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>10</td>
<td>71W</td>
<td>Amending a water access licence to nominate, change or withdraw a water supply work, enabling water to be taken from a different location as part of nominating works in NSW water tagging zones or extraction points in interstate zones in accordance with section 71W of the <i>Water Management Act 2000</i> (NSW)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>11</td>
<td>IDEC</td>
<td>The trade or transfer in NSW of the daily volume of water that may be extracted under an individual water access licence once the commence‑to‑pump thresholds have been reached<br>Note: IDEC is short for individual daily extraction component</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Victoria</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>12</td>
<td>Temporary transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer establishes an arrangement providing for the temporary transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right in Victoria that is:<br>(a) a Vic take and use licence; or<br>(b) a water delivery right; or<br>(c) a capped extraction share</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>13</td>
<td>Limited term transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer establishes a lease arrangement of a permanent eligible tradeable water right that is a water access entitlement in Victoria to a lessee for a fixed period</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Queensland</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>14</td>
<td>Transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a permanent eligible tradeable water right in Queensland</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>15</td>
<td>Lease</td>
<td>The trade or transfer in Queensland involving the lease of a permanent eligible tradeable water right to a lessee for a fixed period</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>16</td>
<td>Sub‑lease</td>
<td>The trade or transfer in Queensland involving the lease of a permanent eligible tradeable water right to a sub‑lessee for a fixed period</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>17</td>
<td>Seasonal assignment</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a temporary eligible tradeable water right linked to seasonal availability or the specific needs of irrigators during a water year within the meaning of the <i>Water Regulation 2016</i> (Qld)<br>Note: Also known as a temporary transfer</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">South Australia</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>18</td>
<td>Water licence transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a water licence in South Australia</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>19</td>
<td>Transfer WAE</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a water access entitlement in South Australia</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>20</td>
<td>Transfer water allocation</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of an unregulated permanent eligible tradeable water right (other than a water licence) in South Australia</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>21</td>
<td>Tagged trade</td>
<td>The trade or transfer establishes:<br>(a) a tag against a water access entitlement in another State to extract allocation in South Australia; or<br>(b) a tag against a water access entitlement in South Australia to extract allocation in another State<br>Note: Also known as a T.1 or T.2 trade application</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>22</td>
<td>Limited period water licence transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a water licence in South Australia to a transferee for a fixed period</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>23</td>
<td>Limited period WAE transfer</td>
<td>The trade or transfer of a water access entitlement in South Australia to a transferee for a fixed period</td>
</tr>
</table>

Note: For the definitions of ***irrigation right*** and ***water allocation***, see section 4 of the Act.

###### 10 Controlled list—offer status

The following table specifies the values for the controlled list “offer status”.

Note: The values describe the status of a buy or sell offer made in respect of the trade or transfer of a water allocation.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Table: Controlled list—offer status</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Item</td>
<td>Column 1<br><b>Controlled list value</b></td>
<td>Column 2<br><b>Description</b></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>1</td>
<td>Open</td>
<td>The offer is on the market and published on the trading platform</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td>Amended</td>
<td>The offer is changed and remains open with the same offer ID<br>Note: The offer is a special type of open status</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td>Matched</td>
<td>The offer is fully filled and is no longer open</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td>Partially matched</td>
<td>The offer is partially filled and is no longer open</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td>Expired</td>
<td>The offer reached an agreed or pre‑set expiry date and time and is no longer open<br>Note: this value typically implies that the offer is automatically removed by the system on reaching the expiry date and time</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>6</td>
<td>Feasibility failed</td>
<td>The offer failed a feasibility test on the relevant water register</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>7</td>
<td>Cancelled</td>
<td>The offer is cancelled and is no longer open</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>8</td>
<td>Error cancel</td>
<td>The previously provided data record contained an error</td>
</tr>
</table>

###### 11 Controlled list—owner type

The following table specifies the values for the controlled list “owner type”.

Note: The values describe the type of owner of an eligible tradeable water right.

<table>
<tr>
<td colspan="3">Table: Controlled list—owner type</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Item</td>
<td>Column 1<br><b>Controlled list value</b></td>
<td>Column 2<br><b>Description</b></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>1</td>
<td>Private commercial</td>
<td>The type of owner of the permanent eligible tradable water right:<br>(a) is primarily an individual, business or other private entity; and<br>(b) is not an owner type described in item 2 or 3</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>2</td>
<td>Environmental</td>
<td>The type of owner of the permanent eligible tradable water right is primarily an environmental water holder or manager<br>The following are examples of this type of owner:<br>(a) the Commonwealth Environmental Water Holder;<br>(b) a state environmental water holder</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>3</td>
<td>Water corporation</td>
<td>The type of owner of the permanent eligible tradable water right is primarily a statutory water corporation, or utility, responsible for urban water supply or irrigation delivery</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>4</td>
<td>Undefined</td>
<td>The type of owner of the permanent eligible tradable water right is not specified or cannot be determined</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>5</td>
<td>Other</td>
<td>The type of owner of the permanent eligible tradeable water right is not mentioned in items 1 to 4</td>
</tr>
</table>
